{"parentUuid":"f81e5c5d-6976-4310-a942-fd969b553c43","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"92bc5889-7d05-41e9-8cd4-144313e36f5e","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:20:36.355Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"92bc5889-7d05-41e9-8cd4-144313e36f5e","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"4f7a525a-6a29-4592-a83a-de21294532ab","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:20:45.218Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

This **falsifies the simplest fix path Option A** (just-drop-prefix would degrade HH-1 branch logic at lines 320/335). **Option B fix path** (wrap in `bash -c 'VAR=val command'`) is empirically proven at S208 step 5 in bash subshell, but needs ONE MORE verification in Claude Code's actual hook runtime before being applied to the 6 production entries (per L-S204-1 doctrine). L-S49b-4 backlog DRAINED |\n| T8 charter Principle 11 | ❌ NOT-STARTED — CHARTER user-gate | latest ADR D-032 (BC-7); no harness-firing principle |\n\nPer Phase 3.5 plan Hard Rule #8: \"Charter ratification (T4 + T8) requires AskUserQuestion gate — neither track may self-execute the charter edit.\"\n\nT5+T6+T8 are interconnected: **T5 protocol → T6 implements → T8 ratifies**. Bundling them maximizes density per `qa_bundle_all_pending.md` mega-bundle doctrine.\n\n---\n\n## Q1 — T5 charter-tier: Approve harness-health-protocol.md NEW constitution authoring with 12-signal catalog?\n\n**Why-needed**: T5 is the constitution-file authoring step. Locks signal catalog (HH-1..HH-12) for T6 implementation. Per Phase 3.5 plan §T5.2, signals span Stop-firing / UserPromptSubmit-firing / promote-rule-cycle freshness / Auto-detect candidates without companion hook / Tier 1 bloat / dispatch-pending JSONL aging / checkpoint freshness / charter md5 stability / mistake-log freshness / firing-test coverage / .session-hooks.log liveness / project.md ↔ current-execution.md Phase coherence.\n\n**Proposed signal set (HH-1..HH-12) per Phase 3.5 plan §T5.2**:\n- HH-1: Stop hook ≥1 fire/session\n- HH-2: UserPromptSubmit ≥1 fire/non-trivial prompt\n- HH-3: Promote-rule cycle delta <8 sessions\n- HH-4: Auto-detect candidates without companion hook = 0\n- HH-5: Tier 1 bloat ≤8K tok\n- HH-6: dispatch-pending JSONL no `state=pending` >2hr\n- HH-7: Checkpoint freshness <2 sessions or <24hr\n- HH-8: Charter md5 unchanged in non-ratify session\n- HH-9: mistake-log.md last entry mtime within 5min of session-end\n- HH-10: Each priority-1 hook has companion firing-test\n- HH-11: .session-hooks.log mtime within session active duration\n- HH-12: current-execution.md Phase = project.md Phase\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve as drafted (12 signals; binding contract for T6 hook) **(Recommended)** — signal set covers all 3 surfaced empirical-firing gaps (Stop hook 0 fires + promote-rule backlog + Auto-detect orphans) + adds defense-in-depth.\n- **B**: Approve BUT user provides amended outline first — user re-scopes signal catalog before agent authors.\n- **C**: Approve with reduced scope (8 signals; defer 4 to v1.1) — pick which 8 are highest priority.\n- **D**: Defer T5 to Phase 4+ (more empirical evidence needed; close Phase 3.5 with T1+T2+T3+T4+T7 only).\n\n---\n\n## Q2 — T8 charter-tier: Approve charter v1.0 → v1.1 with NEW Core Principle 11?\n\n**Proposed Principle 11 wording (verbatim, per Phase 3.5 plan §T8.1)**:\n\n> **Principle 11 — Harness must self-verify firing, not self-attest existence.** Every hook ships with a companion firing-test; T6 harness-health-self-scan continuously verifies signal-set HH-1..HH-N. Ritual closure of a track is forbidden until empirical-firing evidence is captured.\n\n**Why-needed**: Codifies the meta-anti-pattern surfaced in Phase 2.5 (8/8 ritual GREEN audit but Stop hook 0 fires + promote-rule 6+ session backlog + 20 Auto-detect candidates orphaned). Per Charter § Revision Protocol: \"A core principle proves materially wrong through 30+ hours of real use\" — Phase 2.5 → Phase 3.5 evidence chain (~60K main wasted across 22 ROUTINE-IDLE sessions S148-S170 + 3 user-pushed defect surfaces) qualifies as the \"30+ hours of real use\" evidence.\n\n**48hr cool-down**: Charter Revision Protocol mandates 48hr cool-down before committing change. S173 = proposal author; ratification ≥S174 + 48hr (= ~S180+).\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve verbatim wording + version bump v1.0→v1.1 + 48hr cool-down honored **(Recommended)** — protocol-compliant; T8.3 charter file edit ≥S180.\n- **B**: Approve verbatim + EXPEDITE skip 48hr cool-down — small wording fix justification (not principle reversal); emits via `<system-reminder>` channel (UserPromptSubmit) or stderr (SessionStart)\n- Optimization: cheap-first ordering (file mtime checks before grep on 1MB log); cache results across same-session prompts; {"parentUuid":"ac25ee4a-a493-4986-b079-482028f3817f","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# human-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents reading anything inside `human-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`human-workspace/` is the **human-authored authority** layer. Everything here represents **human decisions, scope, intent, and verification**. Agents READ this directory as authoritative; agents do NOT WRITE here except in one designated channel (`q-and-a/pending/` — where agents post questions for human to answer).\n\nThis separation exists because of a failure mode observed in the orch sister project (orch `decisions/040`, post-mortem CF-DOGFOOD-2): when human and agent share a single workspace without contract, agent silently overrides human-CRITICAL items via technical-defer logic. The user explicitly raised this concern in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_02_init.txt` §1.1.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Who writes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `user_prompt/` | Human's mid-flight scope/idea drops. Naming: `YYYYMMDD_NN_<slug>.txt`. Files are immutable post-creation (audit trail). | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `decisions/` | Human's strategic decisions (e.g., scope-lock, charter-revision-trigger, peer-share authorization). Format: `D-H-<NNN>-<slug>.md`. | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `q-and-a/pending/` | **The ONE channel where agent writes inside human-workspace**. Agent drops Q&A bundles per Grill Maximization doctrine; human responds inline or moves to `answered/`. | **Both** (agent writes new bundles; human writes answers). |\n| `q-and-a/answered/` | Q&A bundles human has answered (with answers inline). Agent reads answers and acts. Archive forever; provenance trail. | **Human moves** (drag from `pending/`); agent reads. |\n| `q-and-a/stale/` | Pending bundles that exceeded their `expected_answer_by` (default 24h, urgent 4h). Auto-moved by SessionStart hook (Track 5). | **Hook moves**; both read. |\n| `notifications/` | Agent push to human — summaries, alerts, milestones. Format: `N-<TS>-<level>-<slug>.md`. Levels: INFO / SUMMARY / ALERT. | **Agent writes**; human reads. |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Agent NEVER writes to `user_prompt/` or `decisions/`.** Permission deny. If something feels like a human decision but the human hasn't written it, dispatch a Q&A bundle into `q-and-a/pending/` instead.\n\n2. **`user_prompt/` files are immutable post-creation.** Agent never deletes, renames, or edits these files. They are the audit trail of human interventions.\n\n3. **Q&A bundle format** (per Track 4 spec): YAML frontmatter (`id`, `topic`, `opened_at`, `expected_answer_by`, `priority`, `related_decisions`, `status`) + question body + answer-fill section. Each bundle = up to 25 questions per Grill Maximization doctrine; split if more.\n\n4. **Agent does not move files between `q-and-a/{pending,answered,stale}/`** — that's human's role (or hook's, in case of stale). Agent only WRITES new pending bundles and READS answers.\n\n5. **Notifications are read-only for human.** Human shouldn't edit `notifications/*` files; if disagreement with notification content, drop a `user_prompt/` instead. Notifications are the agent's account of events.\n\n6. **Provenance pointers**: every `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-<NNN>` should reference its source `human-workspace/user_prompt/<file>` or `human-workspace/decisions/D-H-<NNN>` if the agent's decision derives from human input.\n\n7. **No multi-tenancy in this directory.** Per identity-scope.md (Track 7), stockforge is single-tenant Phase 0-5; if peer share happens it's via git-fork, each peer has their own `human-workspace/` per fork.\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nWhen loading session context (per `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` Track 7):\n\n1. **All `user_prompt/*.txt`** — every prompt, not just most recent. Per orch lesson (decision-040 USER-CRITICAL tier): never silently defer human-CRITICAL items.\n2. **All `decisions/*.md`** — human's strategic decisions are above charter principles in priority; charter is below human (charter was authored by human in the first place).\n3. **Latest `q-and-a/answered/*`** — recent answers update agent's understanding.\n4. **Open `q-and-a/pending/*`** — flag if waiting on human answers.\n5. **Recent `notifications/*`** (last 7 days) — for situational awareness.\n\n---\n\n## Agent Self-Check\n\nBefore writing to `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`, ask:\n- Is this question genuinely something the human must decide? (Charter principle / Charter-affecting / SCOPE-affecting / above ARCH-tier confidence threshold)\n- Have I already grilled this in a recent bundle? (Don't ask twice; don't pollute pending/)\n- Am I bundling per Grill Maximization (target 15-20 questions, max 25)? Or splintering across many small bundles?\n\nIf the answer to any is \"I'm not sure\" — DON'T write the bundle. Either answer it yourself with charter-principle reasoning + log decision, or wait until ambiguity clears.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"83f39338-b9c7-4d3d-91cb-b8b327426be2","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:22:22.447Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

failure_mode codes)\n#   agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens          (real-transcript token count)\n#   agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log          (session_id source)\n#   agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-*-N.md        (session_n parser)\n#\n# Output: agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/sessions-rollup.tsv (append; header on first run)\n# Schema: session_n  session_id  ts_utc  tokens_real  tools_used  subagents  failure_codes  wall_min\n#\n# Trigger: Stop hook (Claude Code session end).\n\nset -uo pipefail\n\nROOT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nSA_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/self-awareness\"\nROLLUP=\"$SA_DIR/sessions-rollup.tsv\"\nLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nDISPATCH=\"$MEM_DIR/dispatch.jsonl\"\nCOMP_TEL=\"$MEM_DIR/component-telemetry.jsonl\"\nTRANS_TOK=\"$MEM_DIR/.transcript-tokens\"\nHOOKS_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nSESSIONS_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/sessions\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$SA_DIR\"\n\nts_utc=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\n\n# session_n — latest sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md filename → N (or 0 if none)\nsession_n=0\nif [[ -d \"$SESSIONS_DIR\" ]]; then\n    latest_session=$(ls -1 \"$SESSIONS_DIR\" 2>/dev/null | grep -E '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-session-[0-9]+\\.md$' | sed -E 's/.*session-([0-9]+)\\.md$/\\1/' | sort -n | tail -1)\n    if [[ -n \"$latest_session\" ]]; then\n        session_n=\"$latest_session\"\n    fi\nfi\n\n# session_id — last UUID-shaped token in .session-hooks.log (or \"unknown\")\nsession_id=\"unknown\"\nif [[ -r \"$HOOKS_LOG\" ]]; then\n    sid=$(grep -oE '[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}' \"$HOOKS_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1)\n    if [[ -n \"$sid\" ]]; then\n        session_id=\"$sid\"\n    fi\nfi\n\n# tokens_real — last line of .transcript-tokens (numeric; default 0)\ntokens_real=0\nif [[ -r \"$TRANS_TOK\" ]]; then\n    last=$(tail -1 \"$TRANS_TOK\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n    if [[ \"$last\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then\n        tokens_real=\"$last\"\n    fi\nfi\n\n# tools_used — count lines in component-telemetry.jsonl (proxy for tool-call count)\ntools_used=0\nif [[ -r \"$COMP_TEL\" ]]; then\n    tools_used=$(wc -l < \"$COMP_TEL\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n    [[ \"$tools_used\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || tools_used=0\nfi\n\n# subagents — DISPATCHED events in dispatch.jsonl\nsubagents=0\nif [[ -r \"$DISPATCH\" ]]; then\n    subagents=$(grep -c '\"event\":\"DISPATCHED\"' \"$DISPATCH\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nfi\n\n# failure_codes — comma-separated A:N,B:M,C:N,... from non-null failure_mode in component-telemetry.jsonl\nfailure_codes=\"-\"\nif [[ -r \"$COMP_TEL\" ]]; then\n    failure_codes=$(awk '\n        match($0, /\"failure_mode\":\"[^\"]+\"/) {\n            f = substr($0, RSTART+16, RLENGTH-17)\n            counts[f]++\n        }\n        END {\n            n = 0\n            out = \"\"\n            for (k in counts) {\n                if (n > 0) out = out \",\"\n                out = out k \":\" counts[k]\n                n++\n            }\n            print (n == 0 ? \"-\" : out)\n        }\n    ' \"$COMP_TEL\" 2>/dev/null)\n    [[ -z \"$failure_codes\" ]] && failure_codes=\"-\"\nfi\n\n# wall_min — minutes elapsed since dispatch.jsonl earliest entry (best-effort; 0 if N/A)\nwall_min=0\nif [[ -r \"$DISPATCH\" && -s \"$DISPATCH\" ]]; then\n    first_ts=$(head -1 \"$DISPATCH\" 2>/dev/null | grep -oE '\"ts_ms\":[0-9]+' | head -1 | cut -d':' -f2)\n    last_ts=$(tail -1 \"$DISPATCH\" 2>/dev/null | grep -oE '\"ts_ms\":[0-9]+' | head -1 | cut -d':' -f2)\n    if [[ \"$first_ts\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ && \"$last_ts\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then\n        delta_ms=$((last_ts - first_ts))\n        wall_min=$((delta_ms / 60000))\n        [[ $wall_min -lt 0 ]] && wall_min=0\n    fi\nfi\n\n# Header on first run.\nif [[ ! -f \"$ROLLUP\" ]]; then\n    printf 'session_n\\tsession_id\\tts_utc\\ttokens_real\\ttools_used\\tsubagents\\tfailure_codes\\twall_min\\n' > \"$ROLLUP\"\nfi\n\n# Append row.\nprintf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n    \"$session_n\" \"$session_id\" \"$ts_utc\" \"$tokens_real\" \"$tools_used\" \"$subagents\" \"$failure_codes\" \"$wall_min\" \\\n    >> \"$ROLLUP\"\n\necho \"[$(date -u +%FT%TZ)] self-awareness-aggregate session_n=$session_n tokens_real=$tokens_real tools_used=$tools_used subagents=$subagents failure_codes=$failure_codes wall_min=$wall_min\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":23,"oldLines":7,"newStart":23,"newLines":7,"lines":[" MEM_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\""," SA_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/self-awareness\""," ROLLUP=\"$SA_DIR/sessions-rollup.tsv\"","-LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"","+LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\""," "," DISPATCH=\"$MEM_DIR/dispatch.jsonl\""," COMP_TEL=\"$MEM_DIR/component-telemetry.jsonl\""]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c6547d52-8aaf-44da-b560-c27977215fe4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"66900840-ecb4-44a8-8295-810524c4bf08","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"5a3cc714-1f62-424a-adb3-e8be73c8c972","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Ja9t9oC4YjtVhCTaidpUCt","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\sync-tracker-update.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"42a1a54a-41dc-450d-9abd-dc9d348aac09","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:27:14.559Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\sync-tracker-update.sh","oldString":"LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"","newString":"LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# sync-tracker-update.sh — Track 8a Confidence Score System update hook\n# Per D-006 (IMPL-S17-1): bash+awk only; no python/jq/yq/sqlite3 (L-S11-1 portability).\n#\n# Usage:\n#   sync-tracker-update.sh <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\n#\n# If <override_delta> is empty/omitted, the hook looks up `weight_<event_type>` from weights.yaml.\n# This preserves the tunable-weights contract: edit weights.yaml once, all callers pick up new values.\n# Override mode is for testing or non-standard partial-credit events.\n#\n# Categories: LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | SCOPE | DECISION_ROUTING\n# Event types: decision_correctness | charter_match | q_and_a_resolution\n#              drift_signal | decision_correction | decision_revocation | thesis_revocation\n#\n# Behavior:\n#   1. Resolve delta from weights.yaml if not overridden.\n#   2. Append event row to events.tsv (atomic append).\n#   3. Recompute state.tsv from full events.tsv replay.\n#   4. Decrement must_grill_remaining for the affected category if >0.\n#   5. Call sync-tracker-render.sh to regenerate _index.md (best-effort; log if fails).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nROOT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)\"\nSYNC_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker\"\nEVENTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\"\nSTATE=\"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\"\nWEIGHTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/weights.yaml\"\nRENDER_HOOK=\"$ROOT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\nLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nCAT=\"${1:-}\"\nEVT=\"${2:-}\"\nOVERRIDE_DELTA=\"${3:-}\"\nDEC_ID=\"${4:-}\"\nSOURCE_EV=\"${5:-}\"\nREASON=\"${6:-}\"\n\nif [[ -z \"$CAT\" || -z \"$EVT\" ]]; then\n  echo \"[sync-tracker-update] usage: $0 <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\n# Validate category\ncase \"$CAT\" in\n  LANGUAGE|DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS|DESIGN_THINKING|SCOPE|DECISION_ROUTING) ;;\n  *) echo \"[sync-tracker-update] invalid category: $CAT\" >&2; exit 2 ;;\nesac\n\n# Resolve delta: override CLI arg wins; else look up weight_<event_type> from weights.yaml.\nget_weight() { awk -F': *' -v key=\"$1\" '$1==key {print $2; exit}' \"$WEIGHTS\"; }\nif [[ -n \"$OVERRIDE_DELTA\" ]]; then\n  # Per M-S98-1 + M-S101-1 (sync-tracker arg-position recurrence S98+S101 same-day):\n  # validate $3 is numeric (decimal, optional sign) OR empty. Catches misordered calls\n  # where decision_id (e.g. \"sync-grilling-S101\") lands in $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA slot,\n  # which historically appeared to \"succeed\" but corrupted state.tsv silently.\n  if ! printf '%s' \"$OVERRIDE_DELTA\" | grep -qE '^-?[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?$'; then\n    echo \"[sync-tracker-update] invalid override_delta='$OVERRIDE_DELTA' — must be numeric (e.g. 0.2 or -0.5) or empty. See M-S98-1 + M-S101-1 for arg-position pattern. Usage: $0 <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  DELTA=\"$OVERRIDE_DELTA\"\nelse\n  DELTA=\"$(get_weight \"weight_$EVT\")\"\n  if [[ -z \"$DELTA\" ]]; then\n    echo \"[sync-tracker-update] no weight defined for event_type=$EVT; pass override_delta or add weight_$EVT to weights.yaml\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nTS=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"\n\n# Append event (TSV; tab-separated; replace tabs in fields with spaces to keep single line)\nsanitize() { printf '%s' \"${1:-}\" | tr '\\t\\n' '  '; }\nprintf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$CAT\" \"$EVT\" \"$DELTA\" \\\n  \"$(sanitize \"$DEC_ID\")\" \"$(sanitize \"$SOURCE_EV\")\" \"$(sanitize \"$REASON\")\" \\\n  >> \"$EVENTS\"\n\n# --- Recompute state.tsv ---\n# Read initial scores from weights.yaml (category_<NAME>: <int>)\nINIT_LANG=\"$(get_weight category_LANGUAGE)\"\nINIT_DOM=\"$(get_weight category_DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS)\"\nINIT_DES=\"$(get_weight category_DESIGN_THINKING)\"\nINIT_SCO=\"$(get_weight category_SCOPE)\"\nINIT_DEC=\"$(get_weight category_DECISION_ROUTING)\"\n\n# Preserve must_grill_remaining from existing state.tsv (so decrement persists across runs)\nget_grill() { awk -F'\\t' -v cat=\"$1\" 'NR>1 && $1==cat {print $5; exit}' \"$STATE\"; }\nGRILL_LANG=\"$(get_grill LANGUAGE)\"; GRILL_LANG=\"${GRILL_LANG:-0}\"\nGRILL_DOM=\"$(get_grill DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS)\"; GRILL_DOM=\"${GRILL_DOM:-0}\"\nGRILL_DES=\"$(get_grill DESIGN_THINKING)\"; GRILL_DES=\"${GRILL_DES:-0}\"\nGRILL_SCO=\"$(get_grill SCOPE)\"; GRILL_SCO=\"${GRILL_SCO:-0}\"\nGRILL_DEC=\"$(get_grill DECISION_ROUTING)\"; GRILL_DEC=\"${GRILL_DEC:-0}\"\n\n# Reversal protocol: revocation events set must_grill_remaining=5\nRVC=\"$(get_weight reversal_must_grill_cycles)\"; RVC=\"${RVC:-5}\"\ncase \"$EVT\" in\n  decision_revocation|thesis_revocation)\n    case \"$CAT\" in\n      LANGUAGE) GRILL_LANG=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS) GRILL_DOM=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      DESIGN_THINKING) GRILL_DES=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      SCOPE) GRILL_SCO=\"$RVC\" ;;\n      DECISION_ROUTING) GRILL_DEC=\"$RVC\" ;;\n    esac\n    ;;\nesac\n\n# Replay events.tsv to compute current scores + sample counts + last_updated_ts.\nawk -F'\\t' -v il=\"$INIT_LANG\" -v id=\"$INIT_DOM\" -v ie=\"$INIT_DES\" -v is=\"$INIT_SCO\" -v ir=\"$INIT_DEC\" \\\n    -v gl=\"$GRILL_LANG\" -v gd=\"$GRILL_DOM\" -v ge=\"$GRILL_DES\" -v gs=\"$GRILL_SCO\" -v gr=\"$GRILL_DEC\" '\nBEGIN {\n  s[\"LANGUAGE\"]=il; s[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=id; s[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=ie; s[\"SCOPE\"]=is; s[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=ir;\n  c[\"LANGUAGE\"]=0; c[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=0; c[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=0; c[\"SCOPE\"]=0; c[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=0;\n  t[\"LANGUAGE\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\"; t[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n  t[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\"; t[\"SCOPE\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n  t[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=\"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z\";\n  g[\"LANGUAGE\"]=gl; g[\"DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\"]=gd; g[\"DESIGN_THINKING\"]=ge; g[\"SCOPE\"]=gs; g[\"DECISION_ROUTING\"]=gr;\n  cats=\"LANGUAGE DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS DESIGN_THINKING SCOPE DECISION_ROUTING\";\n}\nNR==1 { next }  # skip header\n$2 in s {\n  s[$2] += $4 + 0;\n  if (s[$2] < 0) s[$2] = 0;\n  if (s[$2] > 100) s[$2] = 100;\n  c[$2] += 1;\n  if ($1 > t[$2]) t[$2] = $1;\n}\nEND {\n  print \"category\\tcurrent_score\\tsample_count\\tlast_updated_ts\\tmust_grill_remaining\";\n  n=split(cats, arr, \" \");\n  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) {\n    k=arr[i];\n    printf \"%s\\t%g\\t%d\\t%s\\t%d\\n\", k, s[k], c[k], t[k], g[k];\n  }\n}\n' \"$EVENTS\" > \"$STATE.tmp\" && mv \"$STATE.tmp\" \"$STATE\"\n\n# Decrement must_grill_remaining for the just-affected category (if it was >0 before this event)\n# (Decrement happens AFTER state replay so the recorded grill count reflects post-event state.)\ncase \"$EVT\" in\n  decision_revocation|thesis_revocation) ;; {"parentUuid":"06503113-7c82-4b0a-9c47-c366b6f3d021","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"5a3cc714-1f62-424a-adb3-e8be73c8c972","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Pwc1D6Hk3hobtf4JpaFN8q","type":"tool_result","content":"22\t## Recent Rules (digest; last 5)\n23\t\n24\t### 2026-05-09 (S213): Per-session dispatch-pending sidecar must record completion to silence HH-6 false-positives — L-S213-1\n25\t**Context**: HH-6-DISPATCH-STALE=MEDIUM(stale=1) fired in S213 UserPromptSubmit hook. Empirical L-S204-1 verify-before-trust: stale sidecar `.dispatch-pending-1ed5fa47-...jsonl` tail-1 = `{\"state\":\"pending\"}` for dispatch_id `toolu_015e7x898N6k6Ed1yJvmTgKe`, BUT `dispatch.jsonl` line 240 confirms same dispatch_id COMPLETED+DONE at ts_ms=1778299773684 (3.5min after PreToolUse). Bug: `scripts/hooks/dispatch-jsonl-recorder.sh` SubagentStop branch (line 199-204) appends COMPLETED to global `dispatch.jsonl` but NEVER updates per-session sidecar. Sidecar accumulates \"pending\" entries forever. HH-6's `tail -1 == state=pending` heuristic flags after 2hr → systemic false-positive. D-041 (S181, 2026-05-07) batch-cleaned sidecars but did NOT fix structural cause; S213's stale=1 = 2nd recurrence (AP-23 RED FLAG: \"Refinement-of-rule (lesson-about-lesson) is AP-23 RED FLAG: 2nd instance mandates promote-or-retire\").\n26\t**Rule**: When a hook maintains a per-session state sidecar with lifecycle fields (state=pending/completed/failed), the lifecycle-completion event handler MUST update the sidecar in addition to any global audit log. Otherwise downstream consumers (HH-* checks, observation reports) see stale \"pending\" indefinitely. Pair with: pre-flight cross-check that any new/modified state-tracking hook covers ALL lifecycle transitions, not just open-state.\n27\t**Anti-example**: dispatch-jsonl-recorder.sh PreToolUse appends `state=pending` to sidecar; SubagentStop appends only to dispatch.jsonl (canonical audit) without updating sidecar. Sidecar tail-1 stuck at \"pending\" forever; HH-6 falsely reports stale.\n28\t**Correct example**: SubagentStop branch appends `{\"dispatch_id\":\"...\",\"state\":\"completed\",\"outcome\":\"DONE\",...}` to sidecar after writing COMPLETED to dispatch.jsonl. HH-6's tail-1 check sees the completion line; only genuine pending dispatches (recorder ran PreToolUse but session crashed before SubagentStop) flag stale.\n29\t**Severity**: medium (HH-6 false-positive class; not catastrophic but pollutes harness-health emergency-broadcast signal — degrades calibration of HH-* alerts; fixed at S213).\n30\t**Auto-detect**: yes — firing-test TC5b in `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/dispatch-jsonl-recorder-fire-test.sh` asserts sidecar tail-1 contains `\"state\":\"completed\"` post-SubagentStop. Regression-detect via firing-test suite.\n31\t**Promotion target**: hook (LANDED S213 via 1-line addition to dispatch-jsonl-recorder.sh SubagentStop branch + companion firing-test TC5b assertion; suite 12/12 PASS post-fix). Skill/charter promotion NOT needed — single-hook structural bug, fix is local.\n32\t**Promotion status (S213, 2026-05-09)**: HOOK-TIER LANDED (1-LOC fix to recorder + 12-LOC TC5b firing-test). Stale sidecar manually deleted (audit preserved in dispatch.jsonl line 240). HH-6 expected to GREEN at next firing.\n33\t**Connection to D-041**: D-041 (S181) ratified Option C \"backfill-then-clean-sweep\" treating HH-6 stale=6 as a one-time data hygiene issue; did NOT identify the structural cause. S213 RC: D-041 was a \"treat the symptom\" fix; L-S213-1 is the corrected structural prevention. AP-23 cheapest-by-RISK: 2nd-recurrence-class bug ⇒ root fix mandated, not 3rd batch cleanup.\n34\t\n35\t### 2026-05-09 (S208): Bash-inline-env-var-prefix in settings.json hook commands fails on Windows hook runtime — L-S208-1\n36\t**Context**: 3 SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks (`harness-health-self-scan`, `idle-escape-detector`, `phase-status-coherence`) at `.claude/settings.json` lines 200, 204, 208 + 281, 285, 289 used `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=<event> bash \"<path>\"` inline-prefix syntax. ALL three had ZERO real-production emit lines from 2026-05-07 → 2026-05-09 (2 days dormant) — including HH-1, the harness-health emergency-broadcast signal that was supposed to detect such regressions. Firing-tests passed (false confidence) because they invoke from a bash subshell that DOES honor inline-env syntax. Empirical chain S208-S212: PowerShell direct repro raises `CommandNotFoundException`; bash subshell repro succeeds; env-dump diagnostic confirmed `with-prefix` variant DOES NOT execute and `bashc-wrap` variant DOES (with CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT correctly captured); 6 production entries rewritten with `bash -c '...'` wrapper at S211; production verification at S212 12:57:35 — all 3 hooks emitted `state=GREEN`, HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09. Bug fully resolved across 5 sessions, zero false-fix iterations.\n37\t**Rule**: `.claude/settings.json` hook entries' `\"command\":` field MUST NOT begin with bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax `VAR=val command`. The Claude Code Windows hook runtime tokenizes the command string WITHOUT spawning a bash subshell to parse the prefix, so the hook never launches. When a hook needs a command-scoped env var, wrap the entire command in `bash -c '...'` to force bash subshell parsing. Note: Claude Code does NOT natively export CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT on Windows (verified at S210 via env-dump) — falsifies the simplest \"drop the prefix\" fix when downstream code branches on EVENT.\n38\t**Anti-example**: `\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"\"` — silently dormant on Windows; firing-test still passes.\n39\t**Correct example**: `\"command\": \"bash -c 'CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"'\"` — outer process invokes bash explicitly; inline-env-prefix parsed by bash subshell.\n40\t**Severity**: critical (HH-1 emergency-broadcast signal silently non-emitting for 2 days; the meta-detector for harness regressions was itself the regression victim; D-042 SCOPE-tier fix premise refuted; full investigation cost ~5 sessions across S208-S212).\n41\t**Auto-detect**: yes — `scripts/hooks/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh` (Stop hook, shipped S212) greps settings.json for regex `\"command\":\\s*\"[A-Z_][A-Z_0-9]*=` at every turn-end. Firing-test 6/6 PASS.\n42\t**Promotion target**: hook (LANDED S212 + firing-test) + skill (LANDED S213 § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability subroutine in vbw-check.md) + charter (proposal at `agent-workspace/proposals/charter-L-S208-1-settings-portability.md`; pairs with L-S11-1 in proposed `portability.md`).\n43\t**Promotion status (S213, 2026-05-09)**: HOOK-TIER LANDED at S212 (settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh + firing-test 6/6 PASS, wired into Stop chain); SKILL-TIER LANDED at S213 (vbw-check.md § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability ~30 LOC); CHARTER-TIER PROPOSAL written to proposals/ — gated by Cluster C charter Q&A bundle (M-S173-1 deny holds direct constitution writes).\n44\t**Connection to D-042 falsification**: D-042 (S184) reordered continue-injector-spawn idx 18 LAST under the assumption \"spawn-induced chain truncation suppresses HH-1\". Real-production verification at S207 falsified that mechanism: idx 18 fired but idx 17 didn't, proving hooks run in parallel (not sequentially) and the actual cause was the inline-env-prefix at idx 17 itself. D-042 = \"treat the symptom under wrong mental model\" failure (L-S207-1 origin). L-S208-1 = corrected RC. Cluster A `guardian-output-inspect-first` rule observation directly informed the S208 verify-don't-act path.\n45\t\n46\t### 2026-05-09 (S207): SCOPE-tier shipped fix MUST be verified empirically against SCOPE outcome — L-S207-1\n47\t**Context**: D-042 (S184, 2026-05-07) shipped with claim \"spawn-induced chain truncation; reorder spawn LAST → harness-health-self-scan emits at SessionStart\". Real-production `/clear` at S207 entry 2026-05-09 12:08:22 falsified: continue-injector-spawn at idx 18 fired ✓ but HH-1 at idx 17 did NOT emit. `.harness-health-cache-unknown` mtime stuck at 2026-05-07T10:46+07:00 = 2 days NO real-production HH-1 emission. D-042's mechanism hypothesis (spawn = truncation cause) is REFUTED.\n48\t**Rule**: SCOPE-tier shipped fixes MUST be verified empirically against the SCOPE OUTCOME (the user-visible state change), not just the PROXY MECHANISM (the immediate code reorder). Two-metric verification: (1) emission/log line PRESENT in .session-hooks.log post-fix; (2) cache/state file mtime advances post-fix. Either alone is insufficient.\n49\t**Anti-example**: D-042 step 5 deferred \"real production verification\" to \"next user `/clear` event\" with no auto-trigger. Result: 2 days of silent SCOPE non-delivery before falsification surfaced.\n50\t**Correct example**: Schedule a Stop-hook auto-check that greps target cache-file mtime > 24h post-ship → WARN if stale; OR include in promotion-cycle the explicit \"verify SCOPE outcome at first production trigger\" step.\n51\t**Severity**: high (HH-1 is the harness-health emergency-broadcast signal; non-emission means RED-N states undetected at every prompt boundary)\n52\t**Auto-detect**: yes — Stop hook mtime-watchdog on `.harness-health-cache-*` files; WARN if stuck > 24h despite SessionStart events recorded.\n53\t**Promotion target**: hook (mtime-watchdog Stop check ~30 LOC) + skill (verify-empirically extension to vbw-check § Verify-SCOPE-fix-against-outcome ~20 LOC). Charter-tier promotion DEFERRED — bundle with Cluster C if user signals.\n54\t**Promotion status (S213, 2026-05-09)**: STILL PENDING — hook + skill not yet landed; subsumed retroactively by L-S208-1's auto-detect Stop hook (settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh) for the specific harness-health subset, but the GENERAL \"verify-SCOPE-fix-against-outcome\" doctrine remains unimplemented. Note: Claude Code does NOT natively export CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT on Windows (verified at S210 via env-dump) — falsifies the simplest \"drop the prefix\" fix when downstream code branches on EVENT.\n**Anti-example**: `\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"\"` — silently dormant on Windows; firing-test still passes.\n**Correct example**: `\"command\": \"bash -c 'CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"'\"` — outer process invokes bash explicitly; inline-env-prefix parsed by bash subshell.\n**Severity**: critical (HH-1 emergency-broadcast signal silently non-emitting for 2 days; the meta-detector for harness regressions was itself the regression victim; D-042 SCOPE-tier fix premise refuted; full investigation cost ~5 sessions across S208-S212).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — `scripts/hooks/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh` (Stop hook, shipped S212) greps settings.json for regex `\"command\":\\s*\"[A-Z_][A-Z_0-9]*=` at every turn-end. Firing-test 6/6 PASS.\n**Promotion target**: hook (LANDED S212 + firing-test) + skill (LANDED S213 § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability subroutine in vbw-check.md) + charter (proposal at `agent-workspace/proposals/charter-L-S208-1-settings-portability.md`; pairs with L-S11-1 in proposed `portability.md`).\n**Promotion status (S213, 2026-05-09)**: HOOK-TIER LANDED at S212 (settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh + firing-test 6/6 PASS, wired into Stop chain); SKILL-TIER LANDED at S213 (vbw-check.md § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability ~30 LOC); CHARTER-TIER PROPOSAL written to proposals/ — gated by Cluster C charter Q&A bundle (M-S173-1 deny holds direct constitution writes).\n**Connection to D-042 falsification**: D-042 (S184) reordered continue-injector-spawn idx 18 LAST under the assumption \"spawn-induced chain truncation suppresses HH-1\". Real-production verification at S207 falsified that mechanism: idx 18 fired but idx 17 didn't, proving hooks run in parallel (not sequentially) and the actual cause was the inline-env-prefix at idx 17 itself. D-042 = \"treat the symptom under wrong mental model\" failure (L-S207-1 origin). L-S208-1 = corrected RC. Two-metric verification: (1) emission/log line PRESENT in .session-hooks.log post-fix; (2) cache/state file mtime advances post-fix. {"parentUuid":"b1da0407-b864-43b6-a486-9366fc13b1f9","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01MQx1LCezz6MFBf2HivYRa3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014GDLtkLrp1P4btxAfae1WS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\hook-log-path-canonical-detector.sh","content":"#!/bin/bash\n# Stop hook: detects hook scripts that write `.session-hooks.log` to project root\n# instead of canonical `agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log`.\n#\n# Anti-pattern: LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"     (writes to project root)\n# Correct:      LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"     (canonical memory dir)\n#               LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"  (also OK; explicit)\n#\n# Origin: L-S214-1 (S214, 2026-05-09). Two hooks (self-awareness-aggregate.sh,\n# sync-tracker-update.sh) silently wrote to project root for ~10 days, causing\n# split log corpus + a near-miss false-bug-fix during S214 triage of HH-1 HIGH.\n# Pairs with settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh (L-S208-1) — both are\n# harness-portability invariant detectors.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)}\"\nHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=$(date -Iseconds)\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\n\n[ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ] || { echo \"[$TS] hook-log-path-canonical-detector: SKIP=hooks-dir-missing session=$SID\" >> \"$LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Match: LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\" — path-suffix at project root, not memory/\nviolations=$(grep -lE 'LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"' \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\n\nif [ \"$violations\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  files=$(grep -lE 'LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"' \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | xargs -I{} basename {} | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')\n  echo \"[$TS] hook-log-path-canonical-detector: state=WARN violations=$violations files=$files session=$SID (L-S214-1)\" >> \"$LOG\"\nelse\n  echo \"[$TS] hook-log-path-canonical-detector: state=GREEN violations=0 session=$SID\" >> \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":6,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6676,"cache_read_input_tokens":134910,"output_tokens":4102,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6676,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":6,"output_tokens":4102,"cache_read_input_tokens":134910,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6676,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6676},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarXW5oEWF6JuF8zocgmv","type":"assistant","uuid":"46d8e40d-eda7-41f4-97c7-e609d4388ff2","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:31:44.692Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

Two hooks (self-awareness-aggregate.sh,\n# sync-tracker-update.sh) silently wrote to project root for ~10 days, causing\n# split log corpus + a near-miss false-bug-fix during S214 triage of HH-1 HIGH.\n# Pairs with settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh (L-S208-1) — both are\n# harness-portability invariant detectors.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)}\"\nHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=$(date -Iseconds)\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\n\n[ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ] || { echo \"[$TS] hook-log-path-canonical-detector: SKIP=hooks-dir-missing session=$SID\" >> \"$LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Match: LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\" — path-suffix at project root, not memory/\nviolations=$(grep -lE 'LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"' \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\n\nif [ \"$violations\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  files=$(grep -lE 'LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"' \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | xargs -I{} basename {} | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')\n  echo \"[$TS] hook-log-path-canonical-detector: state=WARN violations=$violations files=$files session=$SID (L-S214-1)\" >> \"$LOG\"\nelse\n  echo \"[$TS] hook-log-path-canonical-detector: state=GREEN violations=0 session=$SID\" >> \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"46d8e40d-eda7-41f4-97c7-e609d4388ff2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

exit 1; }\n\nPASS_COUNT=0\nFAIL_COUNT=0\n\nassert() {\n  local name=\"$1\"\n  local expected=\"$2\"\n  local actual=\"$3\"\n  if [ \"$expected\" = \"$actual\" ]; then\n    echo \"PASS $name\"\n    PASS_COUNT=$((PASS_COUNT + 1))\n  else\n    echo \"FAIL $name: expected=$expected actual=$actual\"\n    FAIL_COUNT=$((FAIL_COUNT + 1))\n  fi\n}\n\n# === TC1: violations-present (WARN expected) ===\nTMP1=$(mktemp -d)\nmkdir -p \"$TMP1/scripts/hooks\" \"$TMP1/agent-workspace/memory\"\ncat > \"$TMP1/scripts/hooks/bad-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nROOT_DIR=\"/some/path\"\nLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\necho \"test\" >> \"$LOG\"\nEOF\ncat > \"$TMP1/scripts/hooks/good-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nROOT_DIR=\"/some/path\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\necho \"test\" >> \"$LOG\"\nEOF\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TMP1\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"tc1\" bash \"$DETECTOR\"\nTC1_EXIT=$?\nTC1_LOG=$(cat \"$TMP1/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\" 2>/dev/null)\nassert \"TC1 exit code 0\" \"0\" \"$TC1_EXIT\"\nif echo \"$TC1_LOG\" | grep -q \"state=WARN violations=1\"; then\n  assert \"TC1 emits WARN with violations=1\" \"yes\" \"yes\"\nelse\n  assert \"TC1 emits WARN with violations=1\" \"yes\" \"no [$TC1_LOG]\"\nfi\nif echo \"$TC1_LOG\" | grep -q \"files=bad-hook.sh\"; then\n  assert \"TC1 names violating file\" \"yes\" \"yes\"\nelse\n  assert \"TC1 names violating file\" \"yes\" \"no [$TC1_LOG]\"\nfi\nrm -rf \"$TMP1\"\n\n# === TC2: clean (GREEN expected) ===\nTMP2=$(mktemp -d)\nmkdir -p \"$TMP2/scripts/hooks\" \"$TMP2/agent-workspace/memory\"\ncat > \"$TMP2/scripts/hooks/good-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nROOT_DIR=\"/some/path\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\necho \"test\" >> \"$LOG\"\nEOF\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TMP2\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"tc2\" bash \"$DETECTOR\"\nTC2_EXIT=$?\nTC2_LOG=$(cat \"$TMP2/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\" 2>/dev/null)\nassert \"TC2 exit code 0\" \"0\" \"$TC2_EXIT\"\nif echo \"$TC2_LOG\" | grep -q \"state=GREEN violations=0\"; then\n  assert \"TC2 emits GREEN\" \"yes\" \"yes\"\nelse\n  assert \"TC2 emits GREEN\" \"yes\" \"no [$TC2_LOG]\"\nfi\nrm -rf \"$TMP2\"\n\n# === TC3: hooks-dir-missing (SKIP expected) ===\nTMP3=$(mktemp -d)\nmkdir -p \"$TMP3/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TMP3\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"tc3\" bash \"$DETECTOR\"\nTC3_EXIT=$?\nTC3_LOG=$(cat \"$TMP3/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\" 2>/dev/null)\nassert \"TC3 exit code 0\" \"0\" \"$TC3_EXIT\"\nif echo \"$TC3_LOG\" | grep -q \"SKIP=hooks-dir-missing\"; then\n  assert \"TC3 emits SKIP\" \"yes\" \"yes\"\nelse\n  assert \"TC3 emits SKIP\" \"yes\" \"no [$TC3_LOG]\"\nfi\nrm -rf \"$TMP3\"\n\n# === TC4: ignores firing-tests subfolder (avoid false-positives on test fixtures) ===\nTMP4=$(mktemp -d)\nmkdir -p \"$TMP4/scripts/hooks/firing-tests\" \"$TMP4/agent-workspace/memory\"\ncat > \"$TMP4/scripts/hooks/firing-tests/some-fire-test.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nEOF\ncat > \"$TMP4/scripts/hooks/clean-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nEOF\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TMP4\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"tc4\" bash \"$DETECTOR\"\nTC4_LOG=$(cat \"$TMP4/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\" 2>/dev/null)\nif echo \"$TC4_LOG\" | grep -q \"state=GREEN violations=0\"; then\n  assert \"TC4 ignores firing-tests subfolder\" \"yes\" \"yes\"\nelse\n  assert \"TC4 ignores firing-tests subfolder\" \"yes\" \"no [$TC4_LOG]\"\nfi\nrm -rf \"$TMP4\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"===== firing-test summary =====\"\necho \"PASS=$PASS_COUNT  FAIL=$FAIL_COUNT\"\n[ \"$FAIL_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":6,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6676,"cache_read_input_tokens":134910,"output_tokens":4102,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6676,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":6,"output_tokens":4102,"cache_read_input_tokens":134910,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6676,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6676},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarXW5oEWF6JuF8zocgmv","type":"assistant","uuid":"79e5059b-6f1f-42df-9e8d-c61a38569fe7","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:32:05.661Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

exit 1; }\n\nPASS_COUNT=0\nFAIL_COUNT=0\n\nassert() {\n  local name=\"$1\"\n  local expected=\"$2\"\n  local actual=\"$3\"\n  if [ \"$expected\" = \"$actual\" ]; then\n    echo \"PASS $name\"\n    PASS_COUNT=$((PASS_COUNT + 1))\n  else\n    echo \"FAIL $name: expected=$expected actual=$actual\"\n    FAIL_COUNT=$((FAIL_COUNT + 1))\n  fi\n}\n\n# === TC1: violations-present (WARN expected) ===\nTMP1=$(mktemp -d)\nmkdir -p \"$TMP1/scripts/hooks\" \"$TMP1/agent-workspace/memory\"\ncat > \"$TMP1/scripts/hooks/bad-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nROOT_DIR=\"/some/path\"\nLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\necho \"test\" >> \"$LOG\"\nEOF\ncat > \"$TMP1/scripts/hooks/good-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nROOT_DIR=\"/some/path\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\necho \"test\" >> \"$LOG\"\nEOF\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TMP1\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"tc1\" bash \"$DETECTOR\"\nTC1_EXIT=$?\nTC1_LOG=$(cat \"$TMP1/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\" 2>/dev/null)\nassert \"TC1 exit code 0\" \"0\" \"$TC1_EXIT\"\nif echo \"$TC1_LOG\" | grep -q \"state=WARN violations=1\"; then\n  files=$(grep -lE 'LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"' \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | xargs -I{} basename {} | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')","newString":"# Match: assignment-line `LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"` (not a comment).\n# Anchor `^[[:space:]]*LOG=` excludes doc-comments showing the anti-pattern.\nPATTERN='^[[:space:]]*LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"'\nviolations=$(grep -lE \"$PATTERN\" \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\n\nif [ \"$violations\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  files=$(grep -lE \"$PATTERN\" \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | xargs -I{} basename {} | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')","originalFile":"#!/bin/bash\n# Stop hook: detects hook scripts that write `.session-hooks.log` to project root\n# instead of canonical `agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log`.\n#\n# Anti-pattern: LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"     (writes to project root)\n# Correct:      LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"     (canonical memory dir)\n#               LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"  (also OK; explicit)\n#\n# Origin: L-S214-1 (S214, 2026-05-09). Two hooks (self-awareness-aggregate.sh,\n# sync-tracker-update.sh) silently wrote to project root for ~10 days, causing\n# split log corpus + a near-miss false-bug-fix during S214 triage of HH-1 HIGH.\n# Pairs with settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh (L-S208-1) — both are\n# harness-portability invariant detectors.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)}\"\nHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=$(date -Iseconds)\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\n\n[ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ] || { echo \"[$TS] hook-log-path-canonical-detector: SKIP=hooks-dir-missing session=$SID\" >> \"$LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Match: LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\" — path-suffix at project root, not memory/\nviolations=$(grep -lE 'LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"' \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh 2>/dev/null | grep -v firing-tests | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\n\nif [ \"$violations\" -gt 0 ]; Reference: agent-notes.md § L-S208-1.\n\n---\n\n## Verify-Single-Canonical-Log-Path (L-S214-1 subroutine)\n\n**Why**: Hook scripts that write `.session-hooks.log` MUST resolve the path as `$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log` (i.e., `agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log`). Writing to project-root-relative `$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log` splits the log corpus, leaves consumers (HH-1, HH-2, HH-9, HH-11) reading an incomplete picture, and produces empirical-investigation false-negatives — a near-miss false-bug-fix was prevented this way at S214 (would have shipped an unnecessary settings.json wrapper-fix targeting a non-existent bug).\n\n**Apply when**: authoring or modifying any hook in `scripts/hooks/*.sh` that emits to `.session-hooks.log`; verifying repo-wide invariant during promote-rule cycles.\n\n**Triple-check protocol** (when introducing a new log-emitting hook):\n1. **Path resolution**: confirm `LOG=` assignment uses `$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log` (or equivalent `$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log`).\n2. **Pre-flight grep**: `grep -E '^[[:space:]]*LOG=\"\\$ROOT_DIR/\\.session-hooks\\.log\"' scripts/hooks/*.sh` should return zero matches.\n3. **Empirical smoke**: invoke the new hook once; verify the new line lands in `agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log` (canonical) and NOT in `.session-hooks.log` (project root).\n4. **Companion firing-test**: assert log emit lands in canonical path under fixture environment.\n\n**Anti-example**: `LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"` followed by `echo \"...\" >> \"$LOG\"` — writes to project root, splits corpus.\n**Correct example**: `LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"` (or `LOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"`).\n\n**Auto-detect**: `scripts/hooks/hook-log-path-canonical-detector.sh` (Stop hook) greps `scripts/hooks/*.sh` for the anti-pattern (assignment-line anchored; comments excluded; firing-tests subfolder excluded) at every turn-end. WARN if any violation. Reference: agent-notes.md § L-S214-1.\n\n---\n\n## When VBW Feels Like Overkill"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":357,"cache_read_input_tokens":166389,"output_tokens":1257,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":357,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1257,"cache_read_input_tokens":166389,"cache_creation_input_tokens":357,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":357},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarXt24NaPtuAjFqyrxt6","type":"assistant","uuid":"cf7d6f2c-3f3f-40a3-aecf-32543c7fb92c","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:36:39.287Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"b42c7ac2-0565-4e1c-98b5-03fe11a1aa4c","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
